## Notes

- SK-V14 W11W close state: JSON parse_only 17 / 17 ADMITTED, JSON
  direct_to_struct 17 / 17 ADMITTED, JSON real_typed_struct 17 / 17 ADMITTED,
  and CSS L4 24 / 24 retained as SK-V15 W0 diagnostic broadcast evidence pending W1.
- W11W admits the final six JSON parse_only rows with generated Track 1
  `runtime::generated_json::parse_only`, independent Track 2 structural oracle
  output, strict same-run `parse_only/sonic_rs::Skipper`, and cold
  `profile_direct` evidence at
  `restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v14/research/skv14-W11W-parse-only-memchr.tsv`.
- Direct rows are strict product rows, not digest-plane rows. Typed rows are
  strict typed product rows. CSS L4 rows are diagnostic full-parse
  summary broadcast rows and are not live admissions until W1/W6 proof lands.
- `gate-json --check-results` consumes the SK-V15 W0 manifest above; native
  Rust JSON comparators are same-run strict anchors, and CSS W8R rows are
  diagnostic non-admission evidence.
- Historical SK-V12 campaign close remains PASS-ADMIT via
  `css_l4/declaration_values/direct_to_struct/main`; REDRESS-127; companion
  report `restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v12/research/w1b/skv12-W1b-css-l4-sota.json`.
